- **gadriel-fix**: Apply (or propose) a remediation for a specific Gadriel finding. The finding ID is required and should match the canonical rule code emitted by the scanner — for example `CODE-W1-L1-001`, `CODE-W1-AI-636`, or `CODE-W3-CONFIG-218`. ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/gadriel-fix.md))
- **gadriel-reports**: Generate all Gadriel compliance PDFs (one per pillar plus the umbrella report) ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gadriel-compliance/commands/gadriel-reports.md))
- **gadriel-reports**: Generate the full set of Gadriel compliance PDFs and drop them into `.security/compliance/`. Each of the eight pillars produces its own report (L1 input handling, L2 output handling, L3 data layer, L4 identity, AI, API, container, config) and a top-level umbrella report rolls the per-pillar findings into a single executive summary. ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/gadriel-reports.md))
- **gadriel-scan**: Run a full Gadriel code security scan against the repo (or a scoped path) ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gadriel-core/commands/gadriel-scan.md))
- **gadriel-scan**: Run the Gadriel code security scanner against the current repository, or scope the scan to a specific path supplied as an argument. This is the headline entry point for ad-hoc verification when a developer wants to confirm the repo is clean before opening a PR. ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/gadriel-scan.md))
- **gadriel-status**: Show the current Gadriel pillar verdict and finding counts ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gadriel-core/commands/gadriel-status.md))
- **gadriel-status**: Print a one-screen verdict of the repo's current security posture: the pillar scores (from `.security/pillar-scores.json`), the open finding counts by severity (from `.security/findings.json`), and the headline pass/fail gate that CI will enforce. ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/gadriel-status.md))
- **gadriel-watch**: Start the Gadriel L1 watch daemon for low-latency on-save scanning ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gadriel-scanners/commands/gadriel-watch.md))
- **gadriel-watch**: Start the Gadriel L1 watch daemon, which monitors the working tree for file changes and re-runs the relevant per-file scanners on save. This is the tight-loop developer experience: edit a file, get findings in under a second without manually triggering a scan. ([Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin](https://github.com/Gadriel-ai/gadriel-cursor-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/gadriel-watch.md))

- **gaia-cleanse**: Cleanses memory and auto-loaded files (CLAUDE.md, rules) of duplication with the wiki, contradictions with the wiki, and stale content, then asks one Apply / Discuss / Decline question; on Apply it applies the report, files any out-of-scope problem as a tech-debt issue, then commits, opens a PR, and merges it on a main-branch run like /update-deps. Pass --apply to re-run the apply-and-publish stage against the most recent report. - **gauntlet**: Gauntlet Loop — incremental division of a broad request, one step BEFORE planning.
